ArrayList<String> list = new ArrayList<String>();

    return None

    if hasattr(obj, 'content_'):

def _get_top_level_package(obj):
    """
    Return the top-level package of the given object.  This is the package
    containing the object's __package__ attribute.

    @param obj: The object to get the package for.
    @type obj: L{obj}
    @return: The package containing the object.
    @rtype: L{str}
    """
    if obj.__package__:
        return obj.__package__
    else:
        return obj.__name__

    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):

        return self.wrap(*args, **kwargs)

        if self.args:
